func northstarStandaloneEgg(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, state, id, name, lifecycle, marker string) *northstarEgg {
	t.Helper()
	dir := filepath.Join(state, "eggs", id)
	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	exe, err := os.Executable()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	identity := exec.CommandContext(t.Context(), exe, "egg", "run", "--session-id", id)
	identity.Env = append(f.env, "WT_NORTHSTAR_PID_FIXTURE=1")
	input, err := identity.StdinPipe()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ready, err := identity.StdoutPipe()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := identity.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() { done <- identity.Wait() }()
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		_ = input.Close()
		select {
		case <-done:
		case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
			_ = identity.Process.Kill()
			<-done
		}
	})
func northstarStartPTY(t *testing.T, f *northstarFixture, binary string, cols, rows int, args ...string) *northstarPTY {
	t.Helper()
	master, slave, err := pty.Open()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	before, err := term.GetState(int(slave.Fd()))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := pty.Setsize(master, &pty.Winsize{Cols: uint16(cols), Rows: uint16(rows)}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cmd := exec.CommandContext(t.Context(), binary, args...)
	cmd.Env, cmd.Dir = f.env, f.work
	cmd.Stdin, cmd.Stdout, cmd.Stderr = slave, slave, slave
	cmd.SysProcAttr = &syscall.SysProcAttr{Setsid: true, Setctty: true, Ctty: 0}
	if err := cmd.Start(); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	h := &northstarPTY{t: t, cmd: cmd, master: master, slave: slave, before: before, emu: vt.NewEmulator(cols, rows), changed: make(chan struct{}, 1), done: make(chan error, 1), readDone: make(chan struct{}), artifact: filepath.Join(f.evidence, strings.ReplaceAll(t.Name(), "/", "-")+"-"+strconv.Itoa(cols)+"-"+strconv.Itoa(cmd.Process.Pid))}
	// VT query replies must be drained even if a child asks for capabilities.
	queryDone := make(chan struct{})
	inputPipe := h.emu.InputPipe().(io.Closer)
	go func() { defer close(queryDone); _, _ = io.Copy(io.Discard, h.emu) }()
	go func() { h.done <- cmd.Wait(); close(h.done) }()
	go func() {
		defer close(h.readDone)
		buf := make([]byte, 8192)
		for {
			n, err := master.Read(buf)
			if n > 0 {
				h.mu.Lock()
				h.transcript.Write(buf[:n])
				_, _ = h.emu.Write(buf[:n])
				h.revision++
				h.mu.Unlock()
				select {
				case h.changed <- struct{}{}:
				default:
				}
			}
			if err != nil {
				return
			}
		}
	}()
	t.Cleanup(func() {
		// Exact owned child group only; no process scans or state-derived PID kills.
		select {
		case <-h.done:
		default:
			_ = syscall.Kill(-cmd.Process.Pid, syscall.SIGTERM)
			select {
			case <-h.done:
			case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
				_ = syscall.Kill(-cmd.Process.Pid, syscall.SIGKILL)
				select {
				case <-h.done:
				case <-time.After(time.Second):
					t.Error("owned PTY child did not join")
				}
			}
		}
		_ = master.Close()
		_ = slave.Close()
		<-h.readDone
		// Emulator.Close changes its unsynchronized closed flag. Close the
		// thread-safe pipe first and join the query reader before that change.
		_ = inputPipe.Close()
		<-queryDone
		h.mu.Lock()
		defer h.mu.Unlock()
		_ = h.emu.Close()
		northstarWrite(t, h.artifact+".ansi", h.transcript.String(), 0600)
		northstarWrite(t, h.artifact+".txt", h.screens.String(), 0600)
		t.Logf("PTY screen transcript: %s.txt", h.artifact)
func (h *northstarPTY) expect(label string, after uint64, contains ...string) string {
	h.t.Helper()
	return h.waitScreen(label, after, func(screen string) bool {
		flat := strings.NewReplacer("\r", "", "\n", "").Replace(screen)
		for _, text := range contains {
			if !strings.Contains(flat, text) {
				return false
			}
		}
		return true
	})
}
func (h *northstarPTY) resize(cols, rows int) uint64 {
	h.t.Helper()
	h.mu.Lock()
	rev := h.revision
	h.emu.Resize(cols, rows)
	h.mu.Unlock()
	if err := pty.Setsize(h.master, &pty.Winsize{Cols: uint16(cols), Rows: uint16(rows)}); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := h.cmd.Process.Signal(syscall.SIGWINCH); err != nil {
		h.t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return rev
}
func (h *northstarPTY) exit(input string) {
	h.t.Helper()
	h.send(input)
	select {
	case err := <-h.done:
		if err != nil {
			h.t.Fatalf("PTY child exit: %v", err)
		}
	case <-time.After(5 * time.Second):
		h.t.Fatal("PTY child exit timeout")
	}
	// Darwin revokes the slave when its session leader exits; the master
	// retains the shared terminal attributes for the restoration assertion.
	after, err := term.GetState(int(h.master.Fd()))
	if err != nil || !reflect.DeepEqual(h.before, after) {
		h.t.Fatalf("cooked termios not restored: before=%+v after=%+v err=%v", h.before, after, err)
	}

    25	func phoneAdmin(ctx context.Context, op, id, name string, result any) error {
    26		if !pake.Available {
    27			return pakeUnavailable()
    28		}

    36		}

    38		if json.Unmarshal(b, &control) != nil || control.Port < 1 || control.Port > 65535 || control.WingID != cfg.WingID || control.Token == "" {
    39			return errors.New("invalid main wing pairing control")
    40		}

    43		if e != nil {
    44			return e
    45		}
    46		req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+control.Token)

    48		defer client.CloseIdleConnections()

    73	func pairCmd() *cobra.Command {
    74		var showLink bool

    81	func runPair(ctx context.Context, in io.Reader, out io.Writer) error {
    82		return runPairWithLink(ctx, in, out, false)
    83	}
    84	func runPairWithLink(ctx context.Context, in io.Reader, out io.Writer, showLink bool) error {
    85		lines := make(chan string)
    86		readErr := make(chan error, 1)
    87		go func() {
    88			scanner := bufio.NewScanner(in)
    89			for scanner.Scan() {
    90				select {
    91				case lines <- scanner.Text():
    92				case <-ctx.Done():
    93					return
    94				}
    95			}
    96			e := scanner.Err()
    97			if e == nil {
    98				e = io.EOF
    99			}
   100			readErr <- e
   101		}()
   102		for {
   103			var q phonepair.Invitation
   104			if e := phoneAdmin(ctx, "create", "", "", &q); e != nil {
   105				return e
   106			}
   107			err := showInvitation(out, q)
   108			if err != nil {
   109				_ = phoneAdmin(ctx, "cancel", q.ID, "", nil)
   110				return err
   111			}
   112			if showLink {
   113				fmt.Fprintf(out, "\nOne-time pairing link (keep private):\n%s\n", q.Link())
   114			}
   115			ticker := time.NewTicker(time.Second)
   116			state := "waiting"
   117			showed := ""
   118			expired := false
   119			allowed := false
   120			pairErr := func() error {
   121				defer ticker.Stop()
   122				completed := false
   123				defer func() {
   124					if !completed {
   125						_ = phoneAdmin(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), "cancel", q.ID, "", nil)
   126					}
   127				}()
   128				for {
   129					select {
   130					case <-ctx.Done():
   131						return ctx.Err()
   132					case e := <-readErr:
   133						return e
   134					case line := <-lines:
   135						if expired || state == "cancelled" || state == "used" {
   136							return nil
   137						}
   138						if state == "confirming" && !allowed && strings.TrimSpace(line) == "" {
   139							if e := phoneAdmin(ctx, "allow", q.ID, "", nil); e != nil {
   140								return e
   141							}
   142							allowed = true
   143							fmt.Fprintf(out, "Allowed on %s's terminal. Waiting for the phone to confirm…\n", q.Name)
   144						}
   145					case <-ticker.C:
   146						remaining := time.Until(q.ExpiresAt)
   147						if remaining <= 0 && !expired {
   148							expired = true
   149							fmt.Fprintln(out, "\nQR expired or already used. Press Enter for a fresh QR.")
   150							continue
   151						}
   152						if expired {
   153							continue
   154						}
   155						var status phonepair.PairStatus
   156						e := phoneAdmin(ctx, "status", q.ID, "", &status)
   157						if e != nil {
   158							expired = true
   159							fmt.Fprintln(out, "\nQR expired or already used. Press Enter for a fresh QR.")
   160							continue
   161						}
   162						state = status.State
   163						switch state {
   164						case "paired":
   165							completed = true
   166							fmt.Fprintf(out, "\nPaired %s with wing %s. Use wt devices to manage access.\n", status.Name, q.Name)
   167							return errPaired
   168						case "confirming":
   169							if showed != status.Emoji {
   170								showed = status.Emoji
   171								fmt.Fprintf(out, "\n%s\n%s wants to pair. Enter to allow.\nOnly allow if all 4 emoji match the phone. Ctrl-C cancels.\n", status.Emoji, status.Name)
   172							}
   173						case "cancelled", "used":
   174							if showed != "cancelled" {
   175								showed = "cancelled"
   176								fmt.Fprintln(out, "\nPairing cancelled or QR already used. Press Enter for a fresh QR.")
   177							}
   178						}
   179						seconds := int(remaining.Seconds())
   180						if state == "waiting" {
   181							fmt.Fprintf(out, "\r%d:%02d · Waiting for scan...    ", seconds/60, seconds%60)
   182						} else {
   183							fmt.Fprintf(out, "\r%d:%02d · %s    ", seconds/60, seconds%60, state)
   184						}
   185					}
   186				}
   187			}()
   188			if errors.Is(pairErr, errPaired) {
   189				return nil
   190			}
   191			if pairErr != nil {
   192				return pairErr
   193			}
   194		}
   195	}
   196	
   197	var errPaired = errors.New("paired")
   198	
   199	func showInvitation(out io.Writer, q phonepair.Invitation) error {
   200		place := "this machine"
   201		if runtime.GOOS == "darwin" {
   202			place = "this Mac"
   203		}
   204		fmt.Fprintf(out, "\nPair a phone with wing %s (%s). The phone will be able to see and control the sessions on this machine.\n\n", q.Name, place)
   205		qr, e := qrcode.New(q.Link(), qrcode.Low)
   206		if e != nil {
   207			return e
   208		}
   209		matrix := qr.Bitmap()
   210		// Two QR rows per terminal row; explicit colors preserve quiet zones in either terminal theme.
   211		for y := 0; y < len(matrix); y += 2 {
   212			fmt.Fprint(out, "\x1b[30;47m")
   213			for x := range matrix[y] {
   214				top := matrix[y][x]
   215				bottom := false
   216				if y+1 < len(matrix) {
   217					bottom = matrix[y+1][x]
   218				}
   219				switch {
   220				case top && bottom:
   221					fmt.Fprint(out, "█")
   222				case top:
   223					fmt.Fprint(out, "▀")
   224				case bottom:
   225					fmt.Fprint(out, "▄")
   226				default:
   227					fmt.Fprint(out, " ")
   228				}
   229			}
   230			fmt.Fprintln(out, "\x1b[0m")
   231		}
   232		if q.Relay == "" {
   233			fmt.Fprintln(out, "Local network only. That's fine; nothing else needed.")
   234		}
   235		for _, endpoint := range q.Endpoints {
   236			fmt.Fprintf(out, "Direct endpoint: %s\n", endpoint)
   237		}
   238		fmt.Fprintf(out, "\n%s\n5:00 · Waiting for scan...\nSame Wi-Fi: no account needed. Away from home: needs %s registered once (`wt login`).\n", q.Secret, place)
   239		return nil
   240	}
   241	func devicesCmd() *cobra.Command {
   242		cmd := &cobra.Command{Use: "devices", Short: "List phones paired with this wing", Args: cobra.NoArgs, RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, _ []string) error {
   243			var devices []phonepair.Device
   244			if e := phoneAdmin(cmd.Context(), "devices", "", "", &devices); e != nil {
   245				return e
   246			}
   247			out := cmd.OutOrStdout()
   248			fmt.Fprintln(out, "NAME\tPAIRED AT\tLAST SEEN\tDEVICE ID")
   249			for _, d := range devices {
   250				fmt.Fprintf(out, "%s\t%s\t%s\t%s\n", d.Name, d.PairedAt.Local().Format(time.RFC3339), d.LastSeen.Local().Format(time.RFC3339), d.ID)
   251			}
   252			return nil
   253		}}
   254		cmd.AddCommand(&cobra.Command{Use: "revoke <name-or-device-id>", Short: "Immediately revoke this phone, including live access", Args: cobra.ExactArgs(1), RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   255			if e := phoneAdmin(cmd.Context(), "revoke", "", args[0], nil); e != nil {
   256				if e.Error() == "unknown_device" {
   257					return fmt.Errorf("device %q is not paired with this wing", args[0])
   258				}
   259				return e
   260			}
   261			fmt.Fprintf(cmd.OutOrStdout(), "Revoked %s. Live access closed.\n", args[0])
   262			return nil
   263		}})
   264		return cmd
   265	}

     1	package main
     2	
     3	// The client mode is a disposable phone stand-in, using the same public wire
     4	// protocol as iOS. Credentials and signing keys stay in this process's memory.
     5	import (
     6		"bufio"
     7		"bytes"
     8		"crypto/cipher"
     9		"crypto/ecdh"
    10		"crypto/ecdsa"
    11		"crypto/elliptic"
    12		"crypto/rand"
    13		"crypto/sha256"
    14		"encoding/base64"
    15		"encoding/json"
    16		"errors"
    17		"fmt"
    18		"io"
    19		"net/http"

    21		"time"

    28	type phoneClient struct {
    29		q                                       phonepair.Invitation
    30		endpoint                                string
    31		signing                                 *ecdsa.PrivateKey
    32		pair, device                            cipher.AEAD
    33		emoji, deviceID, credential, connection string
    34		sequence                                uint64
    35		http                                    *http.Client
    36	}
    37	
    38	func marshal(v any) []byte { b, _ := json.Marshal(v); return b }
    39	func (p *phoneClient) wire(r phonepair.Request) (phonepair.Response, error) {
    40		var out phonepair.Response
    41		key, e := ecdh.X25519().GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
    42		if e != nil {
    43			return out, e
    44		}
    45		g, e := auth.DeriveSharedKey(key, p.q.Key, "wt-phone-carrier-v1")
    46		if e != nil {
    47			return out, e
    48		}
    49		payload, e := auth.Encrypt(g, marshal(r))
    50		if e != nil {
    51			return out, e
    52		}
    53		box := phonepair.Request{Op: "phone.box", SenderPub: base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(key.PublicKey().Bytes()), Payload: payload}
    54		resp, e := p.http.Post(p.endpoint+"/phone", "application/json", bytes.NewReader(marshal(box)))
    55		if e != nil {
    56			return out, e
    57		}
    58		defer resp.Body.Close()
    59		if resp.StatusCode != 200 {
    60			return out, fmt.Errorf("carrier status %d", resp.StatusCode)
    61		}
    62		if e = json.NewDecoder(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, phonepair.MaxPayload+4096)).Decode(&out); e != nil {
    63			return out, e
    64		}
    65		if out.Error != "" {
    66			return out, errors.New(out.Error)
    67		}
    68		b, e := auth.Decrypt(g, out.Payload)
    69		if e != nil {
    70			return out, e
    71		}
    72		if e = json.Unmarshal(b, &out); e != nil {
    73			return out, e
    74		}
    75		if out.Error != "" {
    76			return out, errors.New(out.Error)
    77		}
    78		return out, nil
    79	}
    80	func (p *phoneClient) enrollment(op string, fields map[string]any) (map[string]any, error) {
    81		fields["op"], fields["id"] = op, p.q.ID
    82		payload, e := auth.Encrypt(p.pair, marshal(fields))
    83		if e != nil {
    84			return nil, e
    85		}
    86		r, e := p.wire(phonepair.Request{Op: "pair." + op, ID: p.q.ID, Payload: payload})
    87		if e != nil {
    88			return nil, e
    89		}
    90		b, e := auth.Decrypt(p.pair, r.Payload)
    91		if e != nil {
    92			return nil, e
    93		}
    94		var result map[string]any
    95		e = json.Unmarshal(b, &result)
    96		if e != nil {
    97			return nil, e
    98		}
    99		if result["id"] != p.q.ID {
   100			return nil, errors.New("wrong invitation")
   101		}
   102		if credential, ok := result["credential"].(string); ok {
   103			id, _ := result["device_id"].(string)
   104			if result["wing_id"] != p.q.WingID || result["key"] != p.q.Key || id == "" || credential == "" {
   105				return nil, errors.New("identity_mismatch")
   106			}
   107			if p.credential != "" && (p.credential != credential || p.deviceID != id) {
   108				return nil, errors.New("duplicate grant")
   109			}
   110			p.credential, p.deviceID = credential, id
   111			delete(result, "credential")
   112		}
   113		return result, nil
   114	}
   115	func (p *phoneClient) start() (any, error) {
   116		a, am, e := pake.New(true, p.q.Secret, phonepair.Identity(p.q))
   117		if e != nil {
   118			return nil, e
   119		}
   120		defer a.Close()
   121		as := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(am)
   122		r, e := p.wire(phonepair.Request{Op: "pair.start", ID: p.q.ID, Message: as})
   123		if e != nil {
   124			return nil, e
   125		}
   126		if r.Key != p.q.Key {
   127			return nil, errors.New("identity_mismatch")
   128		}
   129		bm, e := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(r.Message)
   130		if e != nil {
   131			return nil, e
   132		}
   133		shared, e := a.Finish(bm)
   134		if e != nil {
   135			return nil, e
   136		}
   137		p.pair, e = phonepair.PairKey(p.q, shared, as, r.Message)
   138		if e != nil {
   139			return nil, e
   140		}
   141		p.emoji = phonepair.EmojiFromShared(p.q, shared, as, r.Message)
   142		return map[string]any{"ok": true}, nil
   143	}
   144	func (p *phoneClient) connect() (any, error) {
   145		key, e := ecdh.X25519().GenerateKey(rand.Reader)
   146		if e != nil {
   147			return nil, e
   148		}
   149		pub := base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(key.PublicKey().Bytes())
   150		p.device, e = auth.DeriveSharedKey(key, p.q.Key, "wt-phone-v1")
   151		if e != nil {
   152			return nil, e
   153		}
   154		r, e := p.wire(phonepair.Request{Op: "device.hello", SenderPub: pub})
   155		if e != nil {
   156			return nil, e
   157		}
   158		b, e := auth.Decrypt(p.device, r.Payload)
   159		if e != nil {
   160			return nil, e
   161		}
   162		var hello struct {
   163			Connection string `json:"connection_id"`
   164			Challenge  string `json:"challenge"`
   165			WingID     string `json:"wing_id"`
   166			Key        string `json:"key"`
   167		}
   168		if e = json.Unmarshal(b, &hello); e != nil {
   169			return nil, e
   170		}
   171		if hello.WingID != p.q.WingID || hello.Key != p.q.Key {
   172			return nil, errors.New("identity_mismatch")
   173		}
   174		p.connection = hello.Connection
   175		p.sequence = 0
   176		digest := sha256.Sum256([]byte("wt-device-v1\n" + p.q.WingID + "\n" + p.connection + "\n" + hello.Challenge + "\n" + pub))
   177		sig, e := ecdsa.SignASN1(rand.Reader, p.signing, digest[:])
   178		if e != nil {
   179			return nil, e
   180		}
   181		payload, e := auth.Encrypt(p.device, marshal(map[string]any{"connection_id": p.connection, "device_id": p.deviceID, "credential": p.credential, "signature": base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(sig)}))
   182		if e != nil {
   183			return nil, e
   184		}
   185		r, e = p.wire(phonepair.Request{Op: "device.auth", ConnectionID: p.connection, Payload: payload})
   186		if e != nil {
   187			return nil, e
   188		}
   189		return p.deviceReply(r, 0)
   190	}
   191	func (p *phoneClient) deviceReply(r phonepair.Response, sequence uint64) (map[string]any, error) {
   192		b, e := auth.Decrypt(p.device, r.Payload)
   193		if e != nil {
   194			return nil, e
   195		}
   196		var result map[string]any
   197		if e = json.Unmarshal(b, &result); e != nil {
   198			return nil, e
   199		}
   200		if result["connection_id"] != p.connection || result["sequence"] != float64(sequence) {
   201			return nil, errors.New("response mismatch")
   202		}
   203		if code, ok := result["error"].(string); ok {
   204			return nil, errors.New(code)
   205		}
   206		return result, nil
   207	}
   208	func (p *phoneClient) rpc(op, id, input string) (any, error) {
   209		p.sequence++
   210		payload, e := auth.Encrypt(p.device, marshal(map[string]any{"op": op, "connection_id": p.connection, "sequence": p.sequence, "session_id": id, "input": input}))
   211		if e != nil {
   212			return nil, e
   213		}
   214		r, e := p.wire(phonepair.Request{Op: "device.command", ConnectionID: p.connection, Payload: payload})
   215		if e != nil {
   216			return nil, e
   217		}
   218		return p.deviceReply(r, p.sequence)
   219	}
   220	func runClient() {
   221		scanner := bufio.NewScanner(os.Stdin)
   222		scanner.Buffer(make([]byte, 4096), phonepair.MaxPayload)
   223		p := &phoneClient{http: &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Second, Transport: &http.Transport{Proxy: nil}, CheckRedirect: func(*http.Request, []*http.Request) error { return http.ErrUseLastResponse }}}
   224		defer p.http.CloseIdleConnections()
   225		for scanner.Scan() {
   226			var cmd struct {
   227				Op, Endpoint, Name, SessionID, Input string
   228				Invitation                           phonepair.Invitation
   229			}
   230			var result any
   231			var e error
   232			if e = json.Unmarshal(scanner.Bytes(), &cmd); e == nil {
   233				switch cmd.Op {
   234				case "init":
   235					*p = phoneClient{q: cmd.Invitation, endpoint: cmd.Endpoint, http: p.http}
   236					p.signing, e = ecdsa.GenerateKey(elliptic.P256(), rand.Reader)
   237					result = map[string]bool{"ok": e == nil}
   238				case "start":
   239					result, e = p.start()
   240				case "prove":
   241					result, e = p.enrollment("prove", map[string]any{"name": cmd.Name, "public_key": base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(elliptic.Marshal(elliptic.P256(), p.signing.X, p.signing.Y))})
   242				case "confirm":
   243					result, e = p.enrollment("confirm", map[string]any{"emoji": p.emoji})
   244				case "status", "cancel":
   245					result, e = p.enrollment(cmd.Op, map[string]any{})
   246				case "connect":
   247					result, e = p.connect()
   248				default:
   249					result, e = p.rpc(cmd.Op, cmd.SessionID, cmd.Input)
   250				}
   251			}
   252			if e != nil {
   253				result = map[string]string{"error": e.Error()}
   254			}
   255			_ = json.NewEncoder(os.Stdout).Encode(result)
   256		}
   257	}

    38	func Listen(ctx context.Context, dir, wingID, name string, relay func() string, private *ecdh.PrivateKey, handler Handler) (*Server, error) {
    39		if !pake.Available {
    40			return nil, nil
    41		}
    42		ln, e := net.Listen("tcp4", "0.0.0.0:0")
    43		if e != nil {
    44			return nil, e
    45		}
    46		port := ln.Addr().(*net.TCPAddr).Port
    47		local := "http://" + net.JoinHostPort(lanAddress(), strconv.Itoa(port))
    48		m, e := New(dir, wingID, name, local, relay, private, handler)
    49		if e != nil {
    50			ln.Close()
    51			return nil, e
    52		}

    54		m.endpoints = func() []string { return append([]string(nil), direct...) }
    55		control := Control{port, randomID(), wingID, m.key, name}
    56		mux := http.NewServeMux()
    57		mux.HandleFunc("POST /phone", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    58			var req Request
    59			if decode(w, r, &req) != nil {
    60				http.Error(w, "invalid request", 400)
    61				return
    62			}
    63			writeJSON(w, m.Box(r.Context(), req))
    64		})
    65		mux.HandleFunc("POST /admin", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    66			host, _, _ := net.SplitHostPort(r.RemoteAddr)
    67			ip := net.ParseIP(host)
    68			if ip == nil || !ip.IsLoopback() || subtle.ConstantTimeCompare([]byte(r.Header.Get("Authorization")), []byte("Bearer "+control.Token)) != 1 {
    69				http.Error(w, "denied", 403)
    70				return
    71			}
    72			var req struct{ Op, ID, Name string }
    73			if decode(w, r, &req) != nil {
    74				http.Error(w, "invalid request", 400)
    75				return
    76			}
    77			var result any
    78			var e error
    79			switch req.Op {
    80			case "create":
    81				result, e = m.Create()
    82			case "status":
    83				result, e = m.Status(req.ID)
    84			case "allow":
    85				e = m.Allow(req.ID)
    86			case "cancel":
    87				e = m.Cancel(req.ID)
    88			case "devices":
    89				result = m.Devices()
    90			case "revoke":
    91				e = m.Revoke(req.Name)
    92			default:
    93				e = failure("denied")
    94			}
    95			if e != nil {
    96				writeJSON(w, map[string]string{"error": e.Error()})
    97				return
    98			}
    99			if result == nil {
   100				result = map[string]bool{"ok": true}
   101			}
   102			writeJSON(w, result)
   103		})
   104		s := &Server{Manager: m, http: &http.Server{Handler: mux, ReadHeaderTimeout: 5 * time.Second, ReadTimeout: 10 * time.Second, WriteTimeout: 30 * time.Second, IdleTimeout: 30 * time.Second, MaxHeaderBytes: 4096}, listener: ln, controlPath: filepath.Join(dir, "phone-control.json")}
   105		if e = saveControl(s.controlPath, encode(control)); e != nil {
   106			ln.Close()
   107			return nil, e
   108		}
   109		s.discovery, e = zeroconf.Register(DiscoveryName(wingID), "_wingthing-phone._tcp", "local.", port, []string{"v=1", "wing_id=" + wingID, "key=" + m.key}, nil)
   110		if e != nil {
   111			log.Printf("wing: Bonjour phone discovery unavailable; scanned IP endpoints still work: %v", e)
   112		}
   113		go func() { _ = s.http.Serve(ln) }()
   114		go func() {
   115			ticker := time.NewTicker(time.Second)
   116			defer ticker.Stop()
   117			for {
   118				select {
   119				case <-ctx.Done():
   120					s.Close()
   121					return
   122				case <-ticker.C:
   123					m.mu.Lock()
   124					m.clean()
   125					m.mu.Unlock()
   126				}
   127			}
   128		}()
   129		return s, nil
   130	}
   131	func (s *Server) Close() {
   132		s.closeOnce.Do(func() {
   133			if s.discovery != nil {
   134				s.discovery.Shutdown()
   135			}
   136			_ = s.http.Close()
   137			s.Manager.Close()
   138			_ = os.Remove(s.controlPath)
   139		})
   140	}
   141	func decode(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, v any) error {
   142		r.Body = http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, MaxPayload+4096)
   143		defer r.Body.Close()
   144		d := json.NewDecoder(r.Body)
   145		d.DisallowUnknownFields()
   146		if e := d.Decode(v); e != nil {
   147			return e
   148		}
   149		var extra any
   150		if d.Decode(&extra) != io.EOF {
   151			return errors.New("trailing data")
   152		}
   153		return nil
   154	}
   155	func writeJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, v any) {
   156		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   157		w.Header().Set("Cache-Control", "no-store")
   158		_ = json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(v)
   159	}
   160	func lanAddress() string {
   161		addrs, _ := net.InterfaceAddrs()
   162		for _, a := range addrs {
   163			ip, n, e := net.ParseCIDR(a.String())
   164			if e == nil && n != nil && ip.To4() != nil && ip.IsPrivate() {
   165				return ip.String()
   166			}
   167		}
   168		return "127.0.0.1"
   169	}
   170	
   171	func saveControl(path string, data []byte) error {
   172		f, err := os.CreateTemp(filepath.Dir(path), ".phone-control-*")
   173		if err != nil {
   174			return err
   175		}
   176		defer os.Remove(f.Name())
   177		if err = f.Chmod(0600); err == nil {
   178			_, err = f.Write(data)
   179		}
   180		if err == nil {
   181			err = f.Sync()
   182		}
   183		closeErr := f.Close()
   184		if err == nil {
   185			err = closeErr
   186		}
   187		if err == nil {
   188			err = os.Rename(f.Name(), path)
   189		}
   190		return err
   191	}

func shortStateDir(scratch string) (string, error) {
	return shortFixtureDir(scratch, "")
}

// ScopedHomeDirs keeps both wings' protected state under the provider's HOME
// write-deny root, alongside (not inside) its writable workspaces and TMPDIR.
// Reserve a one-byte state component before choosing the physical HOME name.
func ScopedHomeDirs(t *testing.T, scratch string) (home, localState, remoteState string) {
	t.Helper()
	home, err := shortFixtureDir(scratch, "l")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = os.RemoveAll(home) })
	localState, remoteState = filepath.Join(home, "l"), filepath.Join(home, "r")
	for _, dir := range []string{localState, remoteState} {
		if err := os.Mkdir(dir, 0700); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	return home, localState, remoteState
}

// ScopedHomePolicy retains the default sandbox and pins the clone-local HOME
// and its ancestors so a provider cannot rename the protected fixture tree.
func startBackgroundWing(childArgs []string) (*exec.Cmd, error) {
	exe, err := os.Executable()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	state, err := config.StateDir()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	state, err = filepath.Abs(state)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	// Remove stale status from previous run
	if err := cmdutil.RemoveIfExists(daemonctl.WingStatusPath()); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("remove stale wing status: %w", err)
	}

	if err := daemonctl.RotateLog(daemonctl.WingLogPath()); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	logFile, err := os.OpenFile(daemonctl.WingLogPath(), os.O_CREATE|os.O_WRONLY|os.O_APPEND, 0644)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("open log: %w", err)
	}

	home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
	if err != nil {
		cmdutil.CloseWithLog("wing log", logFile)
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("resolve user home: %w", err)
	}

	child := exec.Command(exe, childArgs...)

func runWingForeground(cmd *cobra.Command, roostFlag, labelsFlag, convFlag, eggConfigFlag, orgFlag string, allowFlags []string, pathsFlag string, debug, audit, local, vte, localOnly bool) error {
	return rev
}
func (h *northstarPTY) screen() (string, uint64) {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	return h.emu.String(), h.revision
}
func (h *northstarPTY) waitScreen(label string, after uint64, match func(string) bool) string {
	h.t.Helper()
	timer := time.NewTimer(8 * time.Second)
	defer timer.Stop()
	for {
		screen, rev := h.screen()
		if rev > after && match(screen) {
			h.mu.Lock()
			fmt.Fprintf(&h.screens, "\n--- %s ---\n%s\n", label, screen)
			h.mu.Unlock()
			return screen
		}
		select {
		case <-h.changed:
		case <-timer.C:
			h.t.Fatalf("screen timeout (%s):\n%s", label, screen)
		}
	}
}

That operation is read-only. It covers both control-file publication and HTTP-serving readiness after endpoint discovery. Both startup paths invoke it before pairing. The one-second delayed Tailscale fixture exercises the slow discovery path without changing an original timeout or assertion.

The fixture starts the actual main-wing CLI with disposable HOME, state and workspace, with cleanup registered before startup. The Go testpeer uses `--client` against that wing’s real encrypted protocol. Phone confirmation must leave enrollment pending without a device grant; terminal Enter then completes pairing. Inventory, attachment/read, detach and PID checks add requirements. Existing assertions receive actual CLI output.

Its real second-call error satisfies the unchanged `"device"` / `"not paired"` prerequisite. The existing journey’s live-output revocation checks remain intact. Non-CGO handling retains explicit unsupported-pairing behavior without adding skips.
